Acknowledgements
My thanks to many members of the astrology faculty who now
teach the mathematical part of Jaimini astrology, relieving me of the
burden of teaching the initial lessons. For years now, I have been
showing how predictions are given through this system of astrology.
Now part of more valuable pieces of research in jaimini astrology done
by me is being added to this book. It Is why this revised and enlarged
edition has to walt evei' after the earlier edition was exhausted much
earlier than I expected.
Some students went through part of the manuscript and gave some
valuable suggestions. [ must make a spedal mention of Sri J.S. Sethi of
]yotish Acharya class of 1996 second semester who pointed out some
mistakes In the horoscopes in the first edition. Unlike other teachers who
become rude and ill-mannered with such students, I asked him to
prepare an errata for Ihe second edition which he did, appreciating my
request. I found his corrections, all done manually, very helpful and they
have been incorporated In this book . .
Over six hundred other students of the two year course of
astrology, some of whom have tried Ihe methods given in this book and
have given predictions successfully, thanked me, and asked me to share
more of my further refined and more elaborate methods of predictions. II
is true, I have neither done it nor shared It with anyone yel because
unless I am convinced of the moral excellence of a person, I will not be
teaching them as may tantamount to committing a sin of passing on
some secrets which will be a sacrilege.
To my younger brohler K. Subhas ftao, especially, who has now
taken over the responsibility of getting my work published and
enti~e
circulated. He Is ably assisted by his wife K. Vijay lakshml ftao.
Sh. Rajendra Singh and Sh. Bharat Singh also need a mention here since
they take care of the secretarial chores.
The Jai.pur Experience
In Jalpur, I was asked to teach Jaiminl astrology for full three days in
1994. I nol merely did il but asked the students to lest my research and
report the results to me. Six monlhs laler, they called me again and
3

Aclmowledgements Predicting through Jaimini's Chara Oasha .
honoured me with a Gold Medal, proclaiming rather loudly that for three
thousand years no such research had been done on laimini's method of
predictlng through a dasha, in this case, the Chara Dasha.
Mr. Satlsh Sharma who now teaches laimini astrology at laipur got it
tested comprehensively. Three years, thirty years, three hundred years or
three thousand years was not material. They had tested my research on
hundreds of horoscopes and got more than 60% (sixty percent) results
which amazed them, and much faster than they were getting earlier
through the Vimshottari Dasha.
As had happened many times before with me, this aroused petty
iealousy even from those who should have known better, having learnt it
themselves when 1 taught it to them and tried it. I do not bother for such
petty men but they should go through all the ]aimini literature available
and see if there is a single book which shows how to use effectively even
one ]aimini dasha. Mine was a painstaking labour of over twenty flve
years. Here were the petty men who felt jealous, having themselves
produced nothing in the nature of even a tiny testable and replicable
research. Worse, they seemed to lack gratitude which will never qualify
them for any moral excellence as astrOlogers. Most of the astrologers do
not know the difference between manufacturing books and producing
research.
If I had kept the research a secret, I would have been predicting
successfully through Chara Dasha and other ]aimini Dashas. earned a
name for myself but failed to promote laimini astrology which I had
decided to do. It needed courage to do it, but 1 was so confldent of my
success. In ]aipur, some astrologers who were initially hostile to my
research, tested it thoroughly and praised me to the skies. Praise or
blame should make no difference. The research is there before you and
you may try it out yourself.
This book is based on series of articles on laimini's Chara Dasha
which were originally In the Astrological Magazine. It was a romantic
moment for me because I was presenting the most revolutionary
research In laimini ,showing the use of Karakas and of Chara Dasha for
predicting events which was never done by any astrologer ever before.
There is no hint available in the Sutra5 of ]alminl or Karkas on it about the
use of Karakas and the use of the dasha for predicting events.
All this I did single handedly and after testing it for nearly a decade
published it. No laimini scholar ever raised a single technical point for
discussion. Dr. Raman' had enough opportunity from 1991 when it was
published in his own Astrological Magazine till his death on
20 December 1998 t9 discuss it technically. or get it discussed technically.
4

Predi(:ting through Jalmini's Chara Dasha
Having produced no research himself in his more than Sixty years of
astrological career, Dr. Ramiln never developed any keen interest in
astrological research is whilt I know personilily beCilUse of our long
correspondence on many matters.
I must record here thilt this book has been the rilvourite of milny
American astrologers who learnt it from me like Yoginl dasha, tried It and
found it most useful for predictions, more than the Vimshottarl Dasha
often, for Its focussed clarity.
As to the criticism born of }ealousy, a new author writing scientifically
or producing an absolutely original research as I have done in this book,
it should be accepted as that Inevitable part of human nature which must
have existed in the world since God created it.

About the Author
K.N. Rae (KOtc1mrdju Mlrayima /Yo) retired from the Indian Audit
and Accounts Service as Oire<tor General in November 1990. He is the
second of the four sons of the famous journalist of the pre- independence
era. K. Rama Rao, the founder editor of the National Herald and, editor of
more than thirty journals in his long journalistic career. Rao was initiated
into astrology by his late mother, K.Saraswanl Devi, at the age of twelve
in 1943. He reg<uds her as the best astrOloger he has known in two
areas, marriage _,nd children and prashfUI (horary).
Rao was a lecturer in English before Joining the government service
through an aU-India competition in 1957. He Joined the Indian Audit and
Accounts Service from which he retired as Director General In November
1990. More interested in games and spons than in astrology In his
,
youth. Rao won brilliancy prizes in chess competitions and two state
championships In bridge competitions. played ten other games
H~
which is why In his astrological writings also there are references to
games often.
During his service career. he was, the planner. organizer and teacher
of three International courses on Audit of Receipts as a joint director once
and director twice. His Interactions with foreigners have been both on
profeSSional and as,trological levels for more than two decades which is
why he has. as an astrOloger. a large international network of friends. He
went on doing all his fundamental reseClrches in astrolOgy during his
service career because of which he went on collecting horoscopes
systematically in thousands. He has in his possession more than 50.QCX)
horoscopes with ten importClnt events of each individuClI noted with him.
II is perhaps. the largest individual collection of horoscopes any astrologer.
-
in the world has.
The strain of doing astrology as a mission, nOI charging any fee.
except on foreign tours Cllmos! mClde him give up astrology mClny times.
But In December 198 1 he was forced out of his shell to pClrticlpClte in CI
three-day seminar on astrology in Deihl. After this ground breaking
speech. there has been a persistent demand for his astrologlcal artides.
From then onwards he has been sharing with his reClders his original
reseClrches for which he has won worldwide prClise.

AOOut the Au/hor Predicting through Jaimini's Chara Dasha
Between 1993 and 1995. Rao has visited the USA on five lecture
tours. He was the Chief Gues! at the Second Conference of the American
Council of Vedic Astrology in 1993. He was requested to be present in
the Third Conference also In 1994 on the opening day because of the
crowds he would draw. His name was advertised till November 1995
also for the fourth Conference though he had made it clear that he would
not be available anymore for the American conferences.
Since June 1998 he has visited Moscow five times where he taught
astrology through interpreters. It was a great success every time as
reported by the Russian sponsors part of those reported is produced
here.
As a result of his academic approach. he has now more than a
thousand students in India and more than five hundred in the USA and
Russia. He is the Director of Astrology Courses in the Bharatiya Vidya
Bhawan, New Delhi. The teachers on the teaching faculty of the astrology
course in the Bharatiya Vidya Bhawan have, like him, never charged any
fees for teaching which they do in an honorary capacity. What impelled
Rao to do it is well explained in his own horoscope where the lagna and
the 10th lords get combined in the lagna, with an exalted /uplter in the
10th house. All this was foreseen by his /yotish Guru. Yogi Bhaskarananda
of Gujarat whom Rao describes as the last of the Rishi ilscrologers in the
purest classical mould. He had told him that he would have to visit many
foreign countries to give to Hindu astrology the honour, recognition and
dignity which it did not have till then. An American summed up the
impact of the Hrst ever foreign visit of Rao to the USA in 1993 as, " Vedic
ilsrrology before RAo ilnd clfter RAo ".
What different yogis have said about astrology as a Ved,mgel which
he must not give up has been quoted in his book, YogiS. Destiny elnd the
Wheel of Time. Astrology is ill-reputed as a profession because of its
mercenary and exploitative nature. Rao's desire never to turn into a
professional astrologer, has won him thousands of admirers and also
some enemies from the community of professional astrOlogers w ho felt
threatened. when around him there grew up a fine team of more than
tw o hundred academic astrologers like him. for whom astrology is not a
source of living. but a super science to delve into the meaning and
purpose of human life. which is what astrology, as Vedanga. should and
has to be.
Both his melntrel g uru. Swami Paramananda Saraswati. and his
;yo(ish guru, Yogi Bhaskarananda taught him some secrets of spiritual
aSl/ology which are not given usually in any book of astrolOgy. Rao has
reve<lled some of these secrets in his book. YogiS. Destiny and the Wheel

The Need to Write
This Book
Shaareeram Manasam Dukham Yoateetamunoshochati,
Dukhena Labhate Dukham Dwanaryo Cha VlndatJ
(MAHABHArA, SHANT[ PARVA)
He who remembers regretfully the physical and mental agonies of
past years again and again, gets a worry after a worry. He has to suffer
two griefs simultaneously. The society we live In ·ls chaotic, confused

The Need 10 Wrile This Book Predicting through JlIimini's Chara Dasha
Why Jaimini
It has to be explained why I had to write this book. For nearly four
decades, laimini astrology fascinated me but there was no book which
could explain how this subtle system was to be used. I gave it up in
sheer despair. At one stage I thought that Jaiminl astrology would
remain totally out of the range of my astrological techniques. How I got
a break through is explained in the next chapter, Introduction dnd
Dedication. In the meantime, after our teaching was started, laimini
astrology was introduced in the curriculum. How was one to teach this
subject and which book was to be prescribed were two questions about
which the Indian Council of Astrological Sciences had no clear thinking.
Dr. B.V.R.:Iman·s book on the subject, gives no method of using laimini
system for any predictions, because neither he nor his grandfather, has
given any examples in the books they wrote. to show how it could be
done. The inevitable impression one got was thdt jaimini was meant
only or mainly for the calculation of the span of one's life. There was no
book showing how to give predictions through Jaimlni astrology about
marriage, birth children, rise and fall in career and various other
problems.To say that Parashara's Vimshottari Dasha alone was sufficient
was to ignore the fact that Jaimini system IS mentioned in the earlier
chapters of the great book. Brihad Pal<\Shara Horashastra, which has
been translated by astrOlogers. totally ignorant of the Jaimlni astrology.
Their translation is totally 'blind', dat:1gerously misleading. To follow
them is to ruin whatever little astrology one knows.
The Burden and Joy of Teaching
The burden of teaching Jaimini astrology fell upon me here in Delhi.
The result was that outside Delhi, I did not know who was teaching
Jaimini astrology. There was no standardised method. I could do it
because before I started teaching, I had given many predictions to many
people without using the Vimshottari dasha. Batches after bdtches were
shown by me how many predictions could be given through Jaimini's
Chara Dasha. In setting the paper on Jaimini, I had strange experiences.
I showed to my students, very highly educated adults, how even Sthira
Dasha given in Dr. R.:Iman's book was unworkable and, how nearly 8QO.ob
horoscopes will have to be left out if one decided to apply this dasha to
them. One must not remain under an illusion that by becoming a writer
of bool(s on astrOlOgy, one has become an expert astrologer or. an
authority on astrology which no one can ever master. /yotish is the
science of sciences. It begins where all physical sciences reach their
dead end. it illumines what must remain areas of unfathomable darknes::.

Predlcllng through Jaimlnl's Chari Dasha The Need fO Wrife This BooIc
for human being, it provides an explanation as no branch of human
knowledge ever <:.an. Needs exists therefore to make a beginning
somewhere to see how to use jaimini astrology. I decided to start with
Chara dasha and showed both in India and the USA, how both could be
combined, In what I called the composite technique of prediction. At
the end of this edition of this book a detailed example has been given,
stage by stage, how the composite technique works. If In future, I And
someone morally excellent answering the Parashara description of a
worthy disciple, I may teach him or her some secrets I know. But I must
teli here, what I know is so little compared to what has still to be
explored In this subtlest branch of astrology known as Jaimini astrology.
My purpose therefore Is both to familiarise astrologers with a workable
technique of prediction and also withhold something vital. My two
books on Jalmini, the present one and the other on Mandook Dasha can
be a starting point for a fast and sound grasp of a horoscope, with the
elements of destiny In it. To Ignore the element of destiny Is to say that
the Sun does not shine.

Jaimini Rishi
Who was this great !aimini rishi?
The simple answer Is that we have !ieard of him, have rea.d his
great aphorisms caffed the /aimini Sutrds, translated by many scholars,
without Illustrating how actually they are to be used for astrological
predictions. This is the greatest drawback with the books on astrology
written by contemporary Indians. They seem to know what they are
doing yet they know not what they are doing.
Three decades ago I had given up !alminl as hopeless but fruitful,
illuminating but confusing. No one, who claimed to be predicting
through !aimini astrology could give me a single correct instance or
even show a replicable technique. I will not mention the names of
people who wrote books on !aimini but could not even explain most
ordinary laimini techniques of predictions.
Tho\!! Unknown Gurus
I have given in the introduction to the first edition how I discovered
Jaimlni technique accidentally, through God's grace.l In a train, on a
pilgrimage, I found two Andhra pandlts discussing a horoscope through
Jalmlni system in Telugu language which Is my mother tongue. I never
allowed them to know that I knew both astrology and a bit of Jaimini.
Better stili, they did not know that I understood Telugu language. Their
discussions gave me some dues to the Interpreting Jaimini which is not
given In any book. It [s to those unknown gurus that I dedicate this
book. The train reached Its destination and I, could get no further clues.
My efforts to rediscover the Jalminl tradition became very intense after
that. I decided that I had to look at this great system in a different way.
Aware as I am, the greatest secrets of Hindu astrology are stili hidden in
pardmpard (tradition) and are not available In books and are confined
mostly to Andhra Pradesh.
Vemuri Rama Murthy Shastri
I first heard about a very proud Andhra astrologer, torally puritanical,
uncompromising and never doing the Vimshottari Dasha for his
predictions. He confined himself to the birth horoscope, the navamsha
and used only !almlnl dashas, not one but more than thirty. at least. He
knew exactly which dasha was applicable to which horoscope depending
on planetary patterns. His name Vemurl Rama Murthy Shastri and his
Vaidlkl background about which one of his admirers from Andhra

Introduction and DedicatiOn Predicting through Jalmlni', Chara Dash.
and he knew nothing about me, I was, for instance doing the Mandodk
dasha wrongly after reading many laimini books in English and Hindi.
Once I saw how he did It. I corrected myself and tested it on those
horoscopes where this dasha became applicable. The result has been so
extraordinary that I could produce my book, Predicting through
Karakamsh" and laimlnl's Mandook Oitsha and also get It tested in the
dasses of laimini in the Bharatiya Vidya Shawano
I then picked up ten more laimini dashas from his illustrated
readings. Vemuri often had the habit of mentioning why he was using a
particular dasha. I could not keep copies of those written predictions
because my friends could not have allowed me to know some other side
of their lives. I pieced up, bit by bit. how Vemurl could rely so
confidently on laimini and laimini dashas alone. Here are some of them.
1. He hailed from a classical Valdlki Brahmin's family of Andhra
Pradesh and was brought up in Vedic tradition. His father never
allowed him to have English education which he thought was
contaminating. After early training in classical learning. including
the memorization of the Vedas, he learnt astrology which he
started doing brilliantly right from his childhood.
2. His father sent him to Varanasi for higher training in Sanskrit
studies. Impressed by the prodigious Intellect of the young Vemurl,
many Sanskrit pandlts showed him the manusclpts on lalmlnl but
never allowed him to copy them down. He memoriZed them and
had as many as 20.000 sutras or shlokas written down in
manuscript form.
3. He was convinced that laimlnl system was the finest 'and subtlest
branch of asuology and needed sharp mind. keener perceptions
and high spiritual discipline. qualities which he dId not find In
disciples who went to him to learn It In a quick. capsule form
som~
as is the modern habit.
4. His manuscripts were burnt down in a fire In his village Is the one
popular version I have heard from many. But some others told me
that he burnt them himself because he thought there was no
wothy disciple wlth high spiritual excellence and a genuine sense
of gratitude.
Vemurl never recreated the laimini tradition. never had a wIsh
even to do It. Yet from what little I learnt t11rough his readings and
employment of different lalmlnl dashas, I was dapled. A new world of
astrology opened out before me. So my dedication to him Is that of an
Ekalayva who never met Guru Dronacharya.

Part - I
Chapter- J
Distinctive Features of
Jaimini Astrology
Since this is meant to be a primer on lalmlnl Astrology to initiate
an astrologer into some predictive uses of this unique system, only such
preliminary aspects will be discussed, as may be found easy to
understand.
Astrologers following Parashari system of astrology should lirst
note the difference in this system, to get used to what appears to be
totally foreign to their conception and assesment of a horoscope. These
are being mentioned briefly here and will be elaborated in different
chapters, with illustrations.
Karakas
Seven planets (exdudlng Rahu and Ketu) become seven karakas
according to their degrees, in descending order, after exduding the
signs. They are called -
Atmakaraka
Amatyakaraka
Bhratrlkaraka
Matrikaraka
Putrakataka
,
Gnatlkaraka and
barakaraka.
Aspects
•
Aspects in this system are from one rashl to another.
Movable rashls aspect all fixed rashls except the adjacent one; all
fixed rashis aspect all movable rashis except the adjacent one; and all
dual rashls aspect all other dual rashis. In this scheme all rashis aspect
three rashis each.
27

Order of Sub Periods Predicting through Jalmini's Chara Oasha
Dashas
Jaimini dashas are known as rashi-dashas. Unlike the Parashari
system of astrology, where dashas are nakshatra-basecI and are named
after the lordship of the nakshatra, the total dashas are twelve; Aries,
Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio. Saglttarius, Capricorn,
Aquarius and Pisces.
of
The Order Cashas
Out of these twelve dashas. six move in direct manner and six in
indirect manner.
of
The Length Dashas
The maximum dasha of a rashl Is of twelve years and the minimum
is of one year. Here there are a few controversIes, which will be
discussed later.
Yogo
Jaimini's Vogas, the Rala Vogas. the Dhana Vogas, the Arishta
Vogas are different. They will be discussed in the following chapters In
Jaiminl's Vogas.
Karakamsha
The Navarnsha oCcupied by the Atmakaraka (the planet with the
highest degrees) is made use of for Important predictions.
Pada or Aroodha Lagna
like the Karakarnsha, the Pada Lagna is made use of for major
predictions. The Pada Lagna Is as many houses away from the position
of the lagna lord, as the lagna lord is from the lagna.
Upapada Lagna
The Upapada Lagna Is as many houses away from the position of
the twelfth lord. as the twelfth lord Is from the twelfth house.
These nine features with Illustrations shall be explained initially .

Karakas
The  first  lesson  which  must  be learnt  In  jaimini  astrology is about
the karakas. To apply, it follow the steps given below:
1.  Calculate  the  degrees  of all  planets  properly.  if  necessary  upto
seconds.
2.  Tabulate  these planets  (omittlng RAhu  and  Ketu)  from  the highest
to  the lowest degrees.  after excluding the signs, in  a descending
order.
3.  Now starting  from  the highest  degree  to  the  lowest degree  they

Sthirakaraka
Only seven  Karakas are  to be used (though some talk of eight.), It
is  always  safe  to use  the  planets,  Sun,  M oon,  Mars,  Mercury,  Jupiter,
Venus,  Saturn,  Rahu  and  Ketu  also with  their well-known significations.
These  are  known  as  5thirdkardkas. Again  here" distinction  Is  made.  I
. have found after examining thousands of horoscopes that  what $Itaram
Iha has given (pliO) Is quite dependable. The scheme outlined Is-'
I .  The sHonger planet between the Sun and Venus represents  father.
2.  The  stronger  planet  between  the  Moon  and  Venus  represents
mother.
3.  Mars  represents  younger brother,  sisters,  brother-In-law and even
mother.
4 .  Mercury represents uncles and  aunt,  (maternal and paternal).
| 5.  Jupiter represents  |             | paternal grandfather.  |
| ----------------------- | ----------- | ---------------------- |
| 6.  Venus               | represents  | husband.               |
7.  Sdturn represents sons.  (It appears strange to me.)
8.  IYhu represents paternal and maternal male grandparents.

Karakamsha
py
The Navamsha occupied the Atmakaraka is Karakamsha. In the
first horoscope, Venus Is the Atmakaraka and is in the Navamsha of leo.
So leo becomes the Karakamsha.
In the second horoscope. Jupiter Is the Atmakaraka and Is in the
Navamsha of Capricorn. which becomes its Karakamsha.
In the third horoscope, Venus Is the Atmakaraka and Is In the
Navarnsha of Gemini, so here Gemini becomes the Karakamsha.

Chapter-3
Chara Dasha Order
In Jaimini astrology the dashas are of rashis and not of nakshatras
(constellations). Therefore, we speak of dasha of Aries, Taurus, Gemini ...
Pisces etc.
Similar Is the nomenclature of the sub-periods. Here we will
describe a particular period as, for example - Aries mahadasha or Aries
major period and Scorpio sub-period.
The most important prindple to remember is the order of the Chara
dasha. And this order is - six dashas are direct or forward and remaining
six are indirect or backward.
,r-, How is this to be decided? Now follow the logic step by step.
The First Step - Note the ninth house from lagna. From Aries it is
Sagittarius: from Taurus It is Capricorn and so on.
The Second Step - If the ninth house from the lagna is Aries,
Taurus, Gemini, libra, Scorpio or Sagittarius then the dasha order will be
direct or forward.
The Third Step - if the ninth house from the iagna Is of the
remaining rashis, e.g., Cancer, leo, Virgo, Capricorn, Aquarius or Pisces
then the dasha order will be indirect or backward.
Thus the twelve houses of the zodiac get divided into two groups:
the direct group and the indirect group.
The Direct Lagna Group
Aries, leo, Virgo, Libra, Aquarius and Pisces.
Note - Remember Aries, Aquarius, Pisces and the seventh house
from them.
The Indirect Lagna Group
Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Scorpio, Sagittarius and Capricorn.
Note - Remember Taurus, Gemini, Cancer and the seventh house
from them.

w ill  be indirect  order  or  in  the  direct  one,  depending  upon  the  rash!

Aspects
ASpects  in  !aimini  astrology  are  from  one  rashi  to  another  rashi.
There  is  no  mention  of any  planetary aspect  in  laimini.  If a  planet  in a
particular  house aspects  another  planet  in  another  house,  it is still  not
described as  planetary aspect. But if-a rashi  containing a planet aspects
another  rashi  containing  another  planet,  then  it  leads  to  a  pl.met's
aspect on another planet.
It will be explained first theoreticalJy and  then through illustrations.
Three  pr:nciples are  to be remembered to understand Jaiminl's aspects.
1.  The  First  Principle  is  that  the  chard  or movable  signs  (Aries,
sthira or fixed
Cancer,  Libra,  and  Capricorn)  aspect all  signs except the
| adjacent sign. Which  |     |     | means that - |     |     |     |     |
| --------------------- | --- | --- | ------------ | --- | --- | --- | --- |
Aries ...... _. .. aspects  Leo.  Scorpio and  Aquarius (not Taurus).
Cancer ....•... aspects Scorpio,  Aquarius and Taurus  (not Leo).
Libra .......... aspects  Aquarius, Taurus  and  Leo  (not Scorpio).
Capricorn-.... aspects Taurus,  Leo and  Scorpio  (not  Aquarius).
Z.  The  Second  Principle  is  that  the  sthira  or  fixed  signs  aspect
chdrd or movable signs  except the adjacent one,  which can  be written
thus -
Taurus ........ aspects Cancer,  Libra  and  Capricorn  (not  Aries).
Leo ............ aspects  Libra, Capricorn  and  Aries  (not Cancer).
Scorpio  ...... aspects Capricorn.  Aries and  Cancer  (not  Ubra).
Aquarius ....  aspects  Aries.  Cancer  and  Libra (not Capricorn).
3.  The Third  Principle  is  that  dwiswabhdvd  or dual  signs aspect
| each  | other.                            | It can                          | be understood thus - |          |                  |             |               |
| ----- | --------------------------------- | ------------------------------- | -------------------- | -------- | ---------------- | ----------- | ------------- |

Mars (AmK) and Venus (AK) in Taurus aspect Cancer, libra and

The Moon (GK) in libra aspects Aquarius, Taurus and Leo. The

The Venus (AtmakarakaJ is In the Navamsha of Leo. Thus Leo Is the

Illustration Two
Matrikaraka and Amatyakaraka in Taurus aspect Cancer, libra and
Capricorn.

Atmakaraka, Gnatikaraka and Darakaraka in Aquarius aspect Aries,
Cancer and Libra.
Venus the Atmakaraka is in the Navamsha of Gemini hence Gemini
becomes the Karakamsha.
Note - The more you concentrate on these aspects the better and
subtler will be your understanding of a horoscope. These aspects, singly
or doubly ·or more, are like the Vogas of Parashari astrology.
In this book I have given examples to show how these aspects
helped me give some good predictions. In doing it, there is tendency to
confuse Parashari aspects often. When doing laimini forget ParasMri
astrology till you have become an adept.

Calculation of Jalmini Chara Dasha Predicting through Jllmlnl's Chari Oa.h •
.

Special Rules for Calculation of Dasha Period of Scorpio and
Aquarius
I. Scorpio (Direct)
There are two lords of Scorpio: Mars and Ketu. The rules to be
followed are -
(a) If Mars is in Scorpio and Ketu in some other rashi, count upto other
rashl, where Ketu is posited, ignoring Mars,
(b) If Ketu is in Scorpio and Mars in some other rashi, ignore Ketu and
count upto Mars.
(c) If both Mars and Ketu are In Scorpio give full twelve years without
any deduction.
(d) If both Mars and Ketu are In rashis other than Scorpio, then find out
which of the two Is stronger. To decide the strength of the two
follow the points mentioned below -
(i) A planet associated with another planet is stronger than an
unassociated one. In the model horoscope no. 3, Ketu Is
alone while Mars is with the Moon. So Mars Is stronger.
In horoscope no. 2, Ketu is with two planets, while Mars is
alone. So Ketu is stronger.
(ii) If both Mars and Ketu are associated with one planet each as
in the case of horoscope no. 1, one with the higher degrees
will be considered stronger. Here Ketu is stronger.
(III) If Ketu and Mars are alone in the rashis other than Scorpio,
again higher degree will settle their strength.
(iv) If both Ketu and Mats are alone and have same degree and
minutes then decide their strength on the basis of higher
seconds.
2. Aquarius (Indirect)
The same rules as have been given for Scorpio will apply, with the
difference that the dual lords are Saturn and Rahu and the method of
counting is Indirect.
Enough examples will be illustrated in the following chapters.
Notes -
1. Commentators want one extra year to be added where a planet is
exalted and one extra year to be deducted In case of a debilitated
planet. It has given me incorrect results. I proved this in my papers
44

Predicting through Jalmlnl's Char. Dasha Calculation of Jaimini Chara Dasha
and no one has countered me. This is for one simple reason.
Suppose Jupiter is debilitated, in Capricorn, then while counting
the dasha period of Sagittarius we will have to count two years and
make one year's standard deduction and then another year's extra
deduction, which wilt give zero years to Sagittarius. It appears
Illogical as Sagittarius dasha has also to give Its results. If there are
zero years, then Sagittarius will become a non,-functional rashi and
dashas will be of eleven rash is. This appears quite illogical. After
working on thousands of horoscopes and timing events, I found
that no rashi can ever have zero years.
Extending this logic I decided not to add one year extra for
an exalted planet and found my timings accurate as illustrated in
this bool<.
Thus, the maximum a rashi can have is twelve years and the
minimum one year.
2. Dr. P.$, Sastri calculates the fractions of each dasha period of each
rashi. For instance Venus in libra will give twelve years. But if
Venus is at 10 degree. he will calculate one third (four years) as
expired portion and give only eight years to libra. He wrote a
series of artides in the AstrologiCiiI MagdZlne In 1983 in which he
was not applying this. Tho,:!gh he has started applying it now he
has not shown through a sIngle iJlustration that how this calculation
comes out correct.
Therefore. take full years and not fractions thereof.
3. Follow the special rules for Scorpio and Aquarius. These are two
rashls where most astrOlogers commit mistakes.

strugg~.

or 11th positions from each other In the birth horoscope or In the

6. See If any karakas are afflicted. They will create trouble.

How Predictions are made
From  Scorpio whose dasha is  running,  the fifth  house receives the
aspects of the Sun.  Mercury.  Ketu  and Jupiter. The fifth  house being the
house of education,  the native did very well  in studies in this period.
He  appeared  for  the all  India  competitive  examinations  and  was
selected  in  the  sub-period  of Sagittarius.  which  has  from  it  a  strong
tenth  house.  Virgo.  with  the Sun  (government service).  In  the last sub
period of ScorpiO which contains his Darakaraka (Dara means spouse) he
got married.

Early Years of Life
In Chicago and eartter in New York, I met some persons who
objected to my habit of starting any astrological reading beginning with
the early years of their life. The objection is untenable as they who daim
to do psychological reading of any individual must first see the Influences
that shaped the outlook of an individual.
This exercise must always be done to make sure that the horoscope
you are working on Is correct. Hindu astrology which Is so well
developed always starts with the analysis of a horoscope w ith childhood
influences because It Is the most developed and most sophisticated and
refined method of psychological delineation of human character. It is
true that Indian astrologers do not use the Jargon of modern psychology
and refrain from talking like western astrologers - "you must use your
Sun-energy well during the present period", etc.
Hindu astrology Is spiritual first then material, three-dimensional in
its approach because the analysis of a horoscope is always divided,
starting from the ascendant into three triangles -
(I) The first tric1ngle consists of the first, the fifth and the ninth houses
which represent, brahmacharya or llfe of celibacy; the st!<ond, the
sixth and the tenth houses grihc15tha or the life of a house-holder,
or the worldly duties of a man or w oman; the third, the seventh
<'Ind the eleventh houses, vanaprastha or the beginning of
detachment from worldly duties and sincere devotion to the higher
spiritual life, and finally the fourth, the eighth and the twelfth
houses are the houses of sannyclSd or a life of renunciation.
What counselling can be done If an astrologer is not able to
see what energies are getting released and when from the sub
stratum of an indlvidual"s personality?
(ii) The second trinngle is to be divided thus - the first. the fifth and
the ninth houses pertain to dhclnncl or ethical-religious conduct;
the second, the sixth and the tenth houses pertain to artha or the
efforts put in to earn and accumulate wealth; the third, the seventh
and the eleventh houses pertain to kclclma or desires, sensual and
sexual; and the fourth. the eighth and the twelfth houses pertain to
m okshcl or such efforts as are put in to attain salvation.
(iii) The third tr;dngle is a three fold division of the twelve houses and
53

O.,h.
Predictive Techniques Predicting throogh Jalmlnl', Chara
is to be used only in those cases In which an aSlfologer's
concentration is on spiritual questions, Here the first. the second,
the third and the fourth houses are the houses of spiritual efforts to
control extroversion; the fifth, the sixth, the seventh and the eighth
houses are the houses of introvert spirltuc'll practices and finally the
ninth, the tenth, the eleventh and the twelfth houses are the
houses of the blossoming of spiritual practices,
These three triangles are made use of only by adept astrologers to
answer dee'p material and spiritual questions of an individual.
Most of the astrologers are only fortune-tellers or the so-called
counsellors who can do what they do without even a horoscope, In fact
when I told someone who claimed to be doing counselling through
horoscopes without even understanding the meaning of planetary
combinations, that he could do what he was doing by merely holding a
blank piece of paper before his eyes, he felt hurt. I apologised to him,
Nevertheless I stick to that view .

The First Lesson
Childhood
Now see the horoscope of it child born on January 30, 1992 at
07.21 a.m., New Delhi. The lagna lord Saturn and the eighth lord Sun
degrees.
are on the same The Gnatikaraka who represents the six house
and therefore accidents and diseases, Venus is in Sagittarius with Mars
and Rahu and is heavily afflicted. In the Vimshottari dasha it was the
period of Mercury, the sixth lord.
The Chara dasha begins with Capricorn which contains two malefks.

Those who come under the sway of western thinking and Insist, that
man has complete freedom, will be surprised how it can be seen through
Hindu astrology that man's free-will can operate only within his
predestined fate and never outside. But that is not the subject matter
right now.
Time for Education
In India we are used to seeing children going to schools first, then
to colleges and finally for higher education or some specialisation In
middle and upper middle classes mostly. Among the poorer classes
start of working life is generally earlier with some elementary education.
In USA and Europe higher education is unlike India, where proper
education of the child Is given perhaps undue importance. In USA it is
not unusual to come across instances of middle aged.or even old persons
joining colleges late if family burdens are less, which are less, unlike
India where an old man will never join such courses in old age because
he feels socially inhibited.-
Keeping this background in the mind, Chara dasha should be used
to see from early childhood If a person is progressing smoothly in formal
education or there are some breaks .

Predicting through Jaimlnl's Chara Dasha The First Lesson - Childhood
Pause and Think
Now 'pause and think whatever you have read so far. You will have
nmiced the following features:
I . Life is an eternal flow, a flux. In the greatest epic of the world. the
Mahabhafata, Bhishma. the venerable warrior describes our
meetings and partings in life and likens them to logs flowing
downstream, and getting driven in different directions by the
currents of water,
Jaimini's Chara dasha shows it as no other dasha ever can.
2, Have you noticed that your Atmakaraka (AK) is sometimes In one
house and sometimes In a different house from your major period.
Even within one major period, there are always changes manifesting
as sub-periods are changing.
This i' what is called the kaleidoscope of life.
3. If you find such changes visible in not one or two but
horosc~s
in hundreds of them, do you find yourself In a position to accept
that there is some unknown karma which keeps on sprouting when
you expect It the least?
4'. Now combine It with Vlmshottari dasha and seek confirmation of
what you see happening. Does it now convince you that there Is
Destiny?
S. From this stage do you feel convinced that It Is astrology through
its refined predictive techniques alone that can explain the strangest
quirks of life?
6. Does It then not take you to the next step and force you to accept
that you did not know why, what happened to you happened?
7. So now do you get convinced that if astrology is not predictive It Is
not a super-science?
8. It must then lead you into the acceptance of the reality of
transmigration of souls. Is that correct?
9, Which nation or which religion or religions believe in the truth of
the transmigration of souls? You perhaps know the answer.
10. 50 in which country of the world do you think astrology originated.
was developed, respected and used effectively?
11. But there is the lurking fear in your mind that if every event in life
Is predestined, where is the role of free-will?
No one can give a difinite answer to this other than the Creator
himself.
let me tell you something still more Significant which will arouse
your hopes. Even the best astrologer of the world commits enough silly
mistClkes in his readings, When you get CI bCld prediction. do not become

gloomy. Two thoughts should spring up in your mind: the first that the
astrologer may have committed one of his sl11y mistakes and the second
that prayers do work miracles. I have seen that happen In life.
It is for this reason that astrology which Is described as jyotlsha In
India was a compulsory subject of study in the hermitages of great
seers. Brought to the market place now, If astrology has become a
marketable commodity in most of the countries of the world, It Is because
of the corrupting mercenary tendencies of the age of technology and
publicity In which we live.
The need to deliver this sermon arose from the fact that very few
astrologers remember that jyotlshd (astrology) basically ought to remain.
a spiritual-academic disdplir:'e.

Chapter - J J
Love Relationship
and Marriage
Women!
It is the the Prophet who declares. "After me I have not left any
affliction more harmful to men than women."(p591). Women constitute
the majority in Hell according to the Prophet.
- ArUII Shourie's The World oj Fotwos
Hindu Explanation of Brnhma's Creation
The first progeny of Lord Brahma, the Creator, was known as manas
(children born through Lord's resolution). But when the first creation
PU(r;f
started seeking salvation right from the start, the Lord faced a serious
problem which was solved through the advice given by lord Shiva. that
in future, creation should be through sex.
Since then sex has become an adorable. artistic, enjoyable and joy
giving act of humans under Ideal conditions, and a pure act of carnality.
brutall[y and criminality under others.
The Questions They Ask
In traditional s.oc.ietles. parents of children particularly of daughters.
ask when their daughters would get married.
In USA and England It becomes a question about relationship.
Now with homo-sexuality getting legalised. astrologers face an
odder question. male-male or female-female compatibility for the purpose
of marriage or relationships. In the USA a homo-sexual came to me and
asked me to match his horoscope with a gay for marital compatibility.
Eighty persons out of hundred would ask this question, in whichever
way they may word It.
Some Parametel1i in Jaimini Astrology
Among the researches I have produced In laimini astrology. this
had to receive serious attention. I then formulated some parametels
which have worked well in a very large percentage of cases.
63

Love Relalionship and Marriage Predicting through Jaimini's Chara Dasha
Note down the following -
1. The ascendant and the seventh house.
2. The rashi containing Darakaraka and its seventh house.
3. The rashi containing Upapada and its seventh house.
4. The rashi containing the pada of the seventh lord and its seventh
house.
5. Put in the birth horoscope the navamsha of the Darakaraka and also
mark the seventh house from it Call it the darakaraka's navamsha
or use the abbreviation DKN.
6. Now note down where the Rahu-Ketu axis fails and which houses
they are aspecting.
7. Similarly note down the houses being aspected by the Darakaraka.
8. See if the Putrakaraka falls in the fifth house from a dasha period.
An Astrologer's Intellectual Equipment
The great Parashara has used a word, 'uha-poha-patu' as one of the
necessary qualities of an astrologer. meaning that he should be able to
weigh the pros and cons of a situation.
Here comes the greatest difficulty. One cannot become a really
good astrologer unless one has a sound sociological background of the
country which the consultor comes from.
Samples of Astrologer's Ignorance
1. During my visit to USA in November 1993, I as a sports-lover
asked the Vedic astrologers whether they had the horoscope of the
famous woman tennis player, Monica Seles, who had been stabbed
in Germany in a tournament.
"Who is Monica Selesr most of them asked me.
In USA emphasis is laid on counselling. not predictions which
appeared strange to me because an astrologer who cannot predict
can never be a good counsellor. An astrologer should be able to
see what is going to happen in future and against the background
of that knowledge only his counselling can have substance and
utility. At least the dient comes to the astrologer with that
expectation. I was made to listen to some cassettes of the so
called readings given by America's Vedic astrologers. It was not
astrology of Hindus at all. It is a mixture of psychology
predominantly with some jargon of Hindu astrology in it.
2. During my second visit to USA in June 1994 I asked them what
was happening to President Clinton's Health Bill since during my
earlier visit, while giving world predictions. I had hirited at difficulties..
64

Predlctln" thrOl.Jgh Jalmlnl'$ Ch,r, DlI$ha LOvt! Relationship and Marriage
for him. No astrologer could help me. Later a doctor told me what
was happening. How can such astrologers do mundane astrology?
The astrologer must become more and more aware of the
sociology of times and contemporary history to be able to do
astrology well otherwise he will utter only platitudes of
psychology, with which he can escape for sometime and with
some people only. In short, the astrologer must be better informed
and, endeavour not to fall in the category of modern neo-illiterates.
3. I face similar difficulty when questions about marriage and
relationships are asked because I am not married, Relationships or
dating as in the west was my big area of ignorance till some
American women explained it to me .
. Since eighty percent questions people in the west ask are
about relationships, rarely marriage. which seems to have become
a less popular social Institution there, I felt that the question must
be viewed under three heads.
First, the astrologers must know the traditions of the society
concerned.
Second. the attitudes towards marriage and relationships must be
known to the astrologer.
Thirdly, there must be some knowledge of the stability of such
institutions, be it marriage or relationships.
Rnally, one must know the societal changes taking place.
The Case Studies - It Is against this background that some
cases are being discussed here astrologically.

and before twenty five.

uncer.

Rounddbout July 1996 or even before her mdrriilSe dispute Is likely

To SlaTt with. some simple principles of both Parashari and Jalmini
are being taken up. The differences in !aiminl calculations are be1r:'lg
explained as a preliminary Introduction first.
(a) Chara dasha for both male and female sexes is done the same way
and not frnm the seventh In the case of females.
(b) No extra addition Is given for exalted planets and no extra
deduction for debilitated planets.
(c) In the case of Scorpio and Aquarius where there is dual ownership,
a planet associated with more planets is stronger. When Mars and
Ketu or Saturn and Rahu are alone, their strength is decided
according to their longitude. The longitude of Rahu or Ketu has not
been taken in the reverse way.
In the first instalment some marital events are being taken up as
experience has shown me that the majority of astrologers are Interested
in marriage and marriage-related problems.

Summary
The identity of the natives has not been disclosed. I have called it
an over-simplied approach. Actually when a prediction is to be given,
particularly in the tragedy that struck In the third and the fifth illustration,
fuller details should be prepared and cross-checked.
Detailed work in the beginning leads to the habit of looking at an
event from various angles and is more elaborate than a medical
dli\gnosis. When point of saturation is reached, sound predictions can
i\
be made with Ie!>ser details. Then it is like an experienced doctor's
dlnlci\1 di.agnosls. At this stage or at any Intermedii\te stage intuition
helps in getting the right prediction. But even without Intuition, an
astrologer should be able to focus his attention on the right spot in a
horoscope. Thi\t Is the advantage of learning more and more astrological
techniques.
Endnotes
*See the article "Role of Darakaraka in vimshottari Dashas" by
Supriya Jagdish in Advanced Techniques of Astrological Predictions,
Vol. 1

Chapter- 13
Amatyakaraka or
Important Persons
In no commentary of Jaimini has the role of Amatyakaraka, the
planet with the setond highest degree, other than Rahu or Ketu, has
been given any place of importance. I do not recollect ever reading any
article in any astrological magazine discussing the role of Amatyakaraka
in a horoscope. It Is a superstition to think that commentators or
translators of Jaimini Sutras had even elementary grasp of this branch of
astrology. If they had, it would have struck them that jaimini used seven
kardkdS which were meant to be used. If their use was not known,
research should have been done. No such research has been done by
older generation of astrologers at all. I use all Karakas and get veiy good
results. I" this book, I will show the use of some of them only.
Yet In my research in jaimini astrology I found Amatyakaraka to be
the key karaka for giving predictions to important persons.
In the second part of this book I have already given some Instances
outlining the role of Amatyakaraka.
In the third Instalment of my research on lalmlnl which appeared In
the February issue of the Astrological Magazine In 1992 r discussed It. A
reader complained that instead of giving bare horoscopes I should have
given full details. I replied that I was not writing a book and within the
•
space of 2500 words I could not have done that. It is being done now.
No laimini scholar made any technical attack on me while I did s"ow an
innovative approach which was giving results.
The Amatyakaraka
Till the secrets of Nadi amshas are unravelled it will not be possible
for astrologers to make totally accurate predictions from birth to death of
all the prominent events of a man's life. When a consultor puts a
question, concentrating on a portion of his life, astrologers very often
give sound and dependable predictions. This is why astrology continues
to hold sway as the only reliable and brilliant predictive science. The
danger of more quacks and chari tans entering the profeSSion will get
checl(ed to some extent when the elitist classes take to the study of
83

Amatyakaraka or Important Persons Predicting throl.ogh Jaimini's Chara Dash.a
Astrology as seriously as is now happening in the Bharatiya Vidya
Bhawan, New Delhi.
Nadi amshas are the division of a rashi of thirty degrees into one
hundred and fifty parts; according to some astrologers these parts are
again divided Into two parts each, which would give to each rashl three
hundred parts. Predictions made on this basis are infallible. But the
technique is lost at the moment. It will have to be rediscovered and can
be done. I hope. in this age of computers.
The fact that the ancient rishis could go to such finer divisions and
use them for predictions stands proven historically and we have brilliant
instances of such readings recorded. Yet most of the Nadi readers (in
northern India Nadi is known as Bhrigu Samhita) are either fakes or use
books without anything p€'rtaining to astrology. or both.
I have developed my own technique of giving a total view of
anyone's life pattern. I have given some intances in my book. Astrology,
Destiny and the Wheel of Time. and have over two hundred
acknowiedged readings, in writing. with me.
I have tabulated over twenty rajayogas, combinations giving power
and high position, in Jaimini astrology. Most of them have been tested
by me,
Earlier when I wrote these they appeared to be the risk I was
taking. Now, I feel, I am on surer grounds,
I have taken such risks very often with my application of Jaimini
astrology and sometimes given dazzling. out of the way predictions,
and sometimes failed so miserably that I almost decided to give up
astrology. However, my rate of high success in these experiments has
kept up my enthusiasm.
With this introduction. I am posing two questions to Jaimini
scholars,
(il Can the nature of the struggle in one's career be seen? What is
shown as struggle is a struggle only or has it some other meaning?
(ii) When will there be rise and when a fall, in a career full of
vicissitudes?
Before this question is answered let me give ten instances here to
show how it can be seen through the position of the Amatyakaraka in

| (b)  | See how it Is               | aspected:  |                               |             |     |     |     |     |
| (e)  | Then see how                | it Is      | conjunct with other planets.  |             |     |     |     |     |
The  rules that can  be applied through further research  are:
(a)  Placement - If placed  In  kendras  or trlkonas  from  the  Lagna or In
the  11 th house. the career will generally  be smooth while Its level
|      | will depend  | upon other factors.                                    |     |     |     |     |     |     |

(c) Conjunction - The planets associated with AmK reveal a good deal

The rules flowing from these pivotal observations will be just the
same as in the case of any other astrological analysis, except that two
new factors come into play - 'alminl's aspects and karakas,
A series of observations made by me on this basis has helped me
formulate some more dependable. though not hundred per cent
replicable, prindples.
1. A malefic becoming AmK, III-placed and aspected shows a man
who has the dash to push through In favourable periods but not
without a controversial image. Or else such persons may turn out
to be technocrats.
2. A benefic becoming AmK, associated with benefics apparently but
with one of them debilitated and the other. an owner, say of the
eighth house. ends up finally with a broken career and dubious
Image, In unfavourable periods.
3. Invariably, any connection of AmK with the sixth lord, without any
association with the eighth lord indicates a man's ability to struggle
in life in favourable periods. If such an AmK receives the aspect of
Jupiter and another exalted benefic. such men do well and shIne In
Intellectual, artistic, literary flelds, including astrological sdences.
Now I give some examples to Illustrate It.
The Meaning of Amatyakaraka
The literal meaning of Amatya Is "a companion or follower of a
king, a minister". In modern times, a very wide meaning should be
given to It In the political. economic. sodal and other flelds. In giving
predictions to beauracrats. I have generally concentrated on their AmK
and the Chara dasha after first seeing their Vimshottari etc. and have
rarely seen this principle falling. Let this be shown through contrasting
Instances.
Illustration One

debilitated Sun and opposition along the 4-10 axis that he

would not do well professionally. But he had risen in the Moon dasha.
Here the Moon which is the AmK. is aspected only by beneflcs. In
tradng his events I asked him what was the extent of his rise during the
Moon dasha and I narrowed down the perIod further to 1983-84
(starting with libra and continuing in Virgo period) after working out his

What we call rajayogas in astrology should be Interpreted as
combinations capable of conferring such Important and slgnlflcant rise in
life as brings one near the centres of political and administrative powers
as Insider or even as an outsider.
1 cannot help giving such a wide dellnltion as in so many horoscopes
I have seen raJayogas giving administrative powers to persons, but In
private firms: and by virtue of such posltlons they are actively In contact
with administrative and political powers.
In these horoscopes what Is clearly visible is that these persons are
involved in the nex!.!s that ex!:;ts between polltidans, businessmen and
bureaucrats.
The Number of Rajayogas
The number of raJayogas that exist In Jaimlni run Into over thirty
but to test and Illustrate them Is laborious and time consuming. About
twenty of them have tested to be giving results. If they are not

Is there a simple way of looking for them in a horoscope w ithout
much complexity In approach? The answer to this lies In examining
through. to start with, the following:
(a) The combination of the Atmakaraka. with Amatyakaraka. PutrakaraM.
Darakaraka or the fifth lord from Lagr.a. giving us four.
(b) Of the Amatyakaraka with Putrakaraka. Darakaraka or the fifth lord
from the lagna giving us three.
(c) Of the Putrakaraka with Darakaraka or the fifth lord from the lagna
giving us two.
(d) Of the Darakaraka with the fifth lord giving us one.
Looklng at these ten rajayogas not missing. similar Parashari

What have been given in this part of the book are my own
researches of years which I have tested on hundreds of horoscopes.
Naturally I insist with absolute confidence that these researches can
be made use of for good and sound predltions and should be combined
with the Vimshottari dasha also. The reason for this will be explained
under the heading, 'Limitations of the Chara dasha' in the chapter -
'Conclusion and Suggestions'.

crudal tool for a better analysis.
Then there are Aner and Anest tools in which our researches are
giving us brilliant results. They are not being given here for some
reasons - Arst let people get used to the Chara dasha In a simple wa:f;
when they show keen interest only then some of the other researches
can be published. TIll now there existed no book in the world on the use
of the Chara dasha for predictions, just as there existed none on Yogini
dasha till Jhanji and Sharma published the Arst ever book in the world,
and so brilliantly illustrated at that.

Chapter- 15
The Dasha of
the Atmakaraka
When I was writing this series for the Astr%giUl/ MagclZine. I
received a letter from an astrologer: why I had not seen the role of the
sub-period of Mercury, the Atmakafaka of Rajiv GandhI, the former
prime minister of India, who was killed in bomb blast, he asked me. r
d
explained it
~
In August 1990. I shared with readers of the Times of Astrology, an
astrological journal. my research In the most difficult dasha in astrology.
the Kaal Chakra dasha.
With Jaimini's Chara dasha, as I am the first ever astrologer to show
how predictions can be made, so too with the Kaal Chakra dasha I had
done that. To illustrate, I had taken Rajiv Gandhi's horoscope and had
discussed It ten months before Rajiv's death. After the death of
Rajiv Gandhi, newspaper men thought with their usual Ignorance that no
astrologer had predicted this tragiC event. Then a very decent journalist
of the Times of India, Vandana Mandlekar, contacted me and after
examining all the evidences, wrote in the Times of India, june 6, 1991 )
the following:
In all faimess sample these hints ... K.N. Rao analysing Rajiv
Gandhi's horoscope in the August 1990 issue of the Times of Astrology
wrote:
"The Pisces-Sagittarius period (June 6, 1990 to August 20, 1991)
is a period of Simhavlokan, a period of total change in his political
style of functioning ... a time of risks and dangerous dare-devilry, a
catadysmic change which can be both fatal and spectacular". Rao
emphasises that he had detected a primary maraka indicating death)
in the June 1990 to August 1991 period,
Then why did Rao word it so cautiously in his interview to
another magazine in April? ("There will be attemps on the lives of big
leaders" is all he said). He did not want to invite the provisions of the
Anti-Terrorist Act, he explains.
The Terrorist and Disruptive Activities (PHivention) Act ironically
passed by the Rajiv Gandhi government in 1987, has become tilt'
105

The Oasha of the Atmakaraka Predicting through Jalminl Chlrl Dasha
proverbial aunt Sally for astrologers now. The act. for the first time.
lumped stargazers with terrorists to declare that action would be
taken against whoever "predicts. prophesises or pronounces or
otherwise expresses. in such manner a'> to incite. advise, suggest or
prompt. the killing or the destruction of any persons bound by oath
under the Constitution .....
Thus. an astrologer predicting Rajiv Gandhi's assassination would
have been liable to a minimum of seven years' imprisonment.
- The Times of India
Rallv Gandhi 's death was seen by me by applying some other
dashas of laimini (the Sthira dasha, particularly), and also the VimshoUari
dasha. But the application of the Vlmshottari dasha was purely dassical,
not the extensIon of the Atmakaraka of laiminl to the Vimshottarl. '
In my book, Predicting through Karakamsha and /aimlnl's Mandook
D.uha, which was brought after this book r have shown major events of
the life of Rajiv Gandhi. Here also a full length portrait of Rallv Gandhi
will be given at the end.
Yet when an astrOloger wrote to me that r had missed the
important event, I had to remind him and others, that;
1. 1 was (he only astrOloger in India to have predicted it, and one year
In advance, on the basis of l eo lagna of Rajlv Gandhi to which I had
stuck consistently. In a Jetter to the editor of the Astrological
Magazine I wrote that Rallv Gandhi 's lagna had to be only Leo. Yet
for another six years the magazine used Virgo for him.
Z. The Astra/og ledl Magazine always discussed the horoscope of Rajiv
Gandhi giving him Virgo ascendant till the end of 1990. In 1984
the Astrological Magazine discussed this horoscope with wrong
birth time and used Chitra Pilkshil Ayanamsha not Raman's. Then in
199 1 the same horoscope was discussed with a different birth time
and with Raman Ayanamsha. If both horoscopes are compared, the
Karakas get totally mangled.
Then most enigmatically In 1991 , the AstrologlCdl Magazine
switched over to Leo ascendant for Rajiv Gandhi and In March 1991
predicted the end of Rajiv era.
M ost significantly , I chose the Kaal Chakra dasha predict this
lO)
event and not the other dashas because, after checking In the other
dashas r applied this for some very clear reasons -
I . The Kaal Chakta dasha is a combination of the features of Parashara's
nakshatra dashas and Jaimini dashas - the discovery or invention of
a super-geniUS or a great yogi or both, which the great Rishi
P"rashara was.

Predicting through Jaimini Chara Oasha The Dasha of the Atmakamka
2. In this dasha there is reference to Simhavlok.m (the gaze of a lion)
which is represented by the sign Leo which means the lion.
3. The great leap that the [ion takes is a fascinating indicator of
dangers and changes in the Kaal Chakra dasha system.
4. The longest leap that is taken in this system is from Sagittarius to
Aries. Aries represents the ram. sheep etc. Here there is the
description of the lion tal(ing such a leap to pounce on its prey.
5. In Kaal Chakra dasha system Leo and Sagittarius become signs of
great changes. often taken with risks. Inferentially, they represent
great fall also.
In /aimini system also the dashas of these two signs, Leo and
Sagittarius become notdble marks of achieving heights and falls in one·s
Cilreer, and often with risks.
Parashi'ra refers to these in his classic.
The reference to - (a) Leo: (b) Sagittarius: and (c) the Atmakaraka
in !aimlni astrology must be made to detect dangers, but not so blindly
as astrologers all over India do.
• ••
India's most ambitious men live in Delhi and for the success of
whatever they undertake they do not mind not merely consulting but
even exploiting astrologers. The late 5itaram jaipuria once told me that
he had made use of astrologers for effective liaison work. But there is
something much worse ...
The fact is, what Kanchi Shankaracharya told me in October 1986.
political predictions cannot be correct more than fifty percent times. I
would say, considering the poor quality of research done in mundane
astrology and the absence of the right horoscopes of those top leaders
as cause swings in Indian politics, that it is less than thirty percent.
The recent astrological mania in Delhi and in the nearby states is to
predict the Fall of a leader on the basis of the major or sub-period of the
Atmakaraka. The success of a chance prediction is paraded as proof of
their understanding of jaiminL
For years I have worked on the problem and I find it difficult to
accept it as an infallible maxim. Then there is another problem. When
Mahadeva stated it as an aphoism. did he refer to jaimini dasha or the
Vimshottari? 50 the best way is to examine it in both ways and leave it
to the readers to draw their final conclusions. I can give conclusions only
at the end. my conclusions.
Pandit Nehru was in jail for some part of his Moon dasha. his
Atmakaraka. and towards its end headed the interim government to
become the first prime minister of independent India. But Nehru was

Atmakaraka in Sagittarius
One of the favourite tricks of astrologers is to seek an explanation
for their failures behind a classical ptlndple which they may not have
tested themselves. Now one of the best explanations is that If AK Is in
Sagittarius or if the Karakamsha lagna is Sagittarius there will be a fall.
Now what does fall mean? It can mean, let me say from my experience.
any of these three things -
(a) Fall from a height physically, fall from a high position from a hill,
from a top floor or even an aeroplane.
(b) It can mean sudden death from such diseases as cause death.
(c) It can mean assassination or violent death.
But this is not coming correct in ail cases if it is applied as a general
rule to an individual.
I have worked on fourteen different dashas of Jaiminl wtth varying
successes In them and now say confidently about nine of them have
given me hlger percentage of results, In all these dashas what I have
observed is that the results given by Jalminl seem to manifest In the
major or sub--periods.
An Interesting corollary to this prindple Is that I have seen people
making careers in Sagittarius dashas.
Saglttatlus as the koditIlda rashl must bestow position of authority
to allow someone to a position of height. See four contrasting examples
from the same family, the Nehrus.
(a) Pt. Nehru has his AK. the Moon, In Saglttatlus navamsha but he
died of an Illness, not suddenly . .
fb) Indira Gandhi has her AK, Saturn, in Cancer with Capricorn navamsha
and she died a violent death.
(c) Sanjay Gandhi has his AK, Venus, in Libra and Taurus navamsha
and he died:v iolent death.
(d) RaJlv Gandhi has his AK. Mercury. in leo and Sagittarius navamsha
and he died a gruesome violent death. llke his brother and mother
but. unlike his grandfather.

through three examples. Reader can what a potent tool Chara Dasha can
become for good and dependable readings and predictions when the
Vimshottari Dasha and divisIonal horoscopes may be doubtful.
Case One - Fixing the lagno and correct birth time
I have to successive batches of students how the Chara

correct lagna. I will explain it here through an example. Now here the
birth horoscope of an non-Indian woman whose birth time may be 2.40
am or 1.40 am. If you take both and prepare the horoscope. the lagna
and the degrees of the Moon, and consequently the navamsha will
undergo a change. Let me show it by using three vargas simultaneously.
I looked at both horoscopes and decided straightway that the 2.40 am.
horoscope was correct. The same events can be seen on the ba. . ls of the
1.40 am. horoscopes. I am avoiding that tedious exercise here.
Note - I had tried to fix her birth time fast when I had horoscopes
based on both birth times were before me with the Chara and Virr",hottari
dashas w orked out on a computer programme.

5. The next dasha of Makar - 1947 to 1955 with no great planetary

6. Kumbha 1955 to 1966 was good for him to consolidate his
position politically with some notable successes and fallures.
7. Meena 1966 to 1969 with all the rajayogas In the 10th house with
the Amatyakaraka he stood the best chance of becoming US
President. It happened Meencl-Meencl.
8. Mesha 1969 to 1977. Notice here the Atmakaraka In the 10th

term but In the sub-period of Dhanu September 1973 (remember It
for rise and fail) the notorious Watergate Case made him vulnerable
and he could not complete his second term as the President of the
USA.

now a retired person.

erratic in some cases. Till that is rectified I will not disclose it here. But
that may be in one out of thousand cases. I can assure readers that my
method of calculation not given in any book. can be depended upon.
mainly because there is no book ever written by anyone showing how
the Chara dasha could be made use of for timing specific events, as has
been shown in this book. And also because I have tested them.
I have two additional advantages in saying what I am; first, for
fifteen years I proved the soundness of my research by gMng predictions.
which I have already discussed in the papers of mine. Secondly I taught
to many batches .of students of the second year astrology dass, these
techniques and they have tested them, openly in my dasses. Many
students of past batches have been reporting to me that they have been
using these methods for successful predictions themselves.
By now. I h<we given predictions to many thousands In India. USA
and England. the places I visited in recent years. without looking at the
Vimshottari dasha at all, successfully, tape·recorded or typed predictions
on the basis of laiminl astrology. But no one can remain complacent and
take the poSition that he has uttered the last word on the subject, or on
the technique of an astrological prediction because there will have to be
additions and alternations. as newer research will be done, as is bound
to happen now.
Karakas
To the modification I have made to the calculations of the Chara
dasha. I can say with utmost confidence. that the meaning of Karakas as
[ have demonstrated, have been my most satisfying pieces of research in
Jalmlnl astrology. I have used the Karakas very successfully for various
areas of horoscopes, with fine shades of meanings emerging out every
time r looked into the depths of a horoscope. Most gratifying has been
the results obtained by our students over a period, now. of ten years. in
the use of these karakas, as expansively as they are being done by us.
These are the areas In which our students are bound to produce super·
brllJlant results In future and surpass my generation. Some, a very very
small number in the generations younger to mine, are better researchers.
They have yet to come out with a fundamental and original research and
not be satisfied with routine writing to be able to establish astrology as
a super·sclence In the next century.
Padas
Jha
Sri Sitaram shows a method 0( doing the padas for all the
twelve houses by referring to some exceptions. I tried that and found it

misleading. Then I found the south Indian method of preparing padas
more rewarding and have used them also, but sparingly, for predictions.
limitations of the Chara. Dasha
A question should be posed. If two persons are born on the same
day with same karakas, same ascendant but with the degrees of the
lagna being different. will the readings for both of them be the same,
astrologically. The answer to It Is, yes.
What then is the solution? It can be answered thus -
(I) In the case of a person whose background is known, to whom
predictions have been given earlier, there Is no problem. This case
resembles a doctor's familiarity with the medical history of an old
and famlllar patient.
(2) In the case of a new person, predictions can be given, after
verifying his past, with the astrologer himself asking the questions
and not his client reeling off his story of life.
(3) To the further question, If there Is a slight difference only In the
degrees of the lagna, how should an astrologer deal with the
answer, Is very dear. There are many subtle and finer dashas,
specific to the planetary patterns of a horoscope both in Parasharl
and Jalmlni astrology.
The subtle divisions made in Vedic astrology through different
dasha systems, through divisional horoscopes meet the challenge of any
situation.
We must always keep reminding ourselves that the surviving
astrological legacy we have Is only a fragment. Then we have frauds
galore In the world of astrology.
Other dashas of Jaimini
Though my major success, after me use of the Karakas, has been In
the use of many other dashas of Jalminl, I do not propose to write about
them, as they can be misused by unethical and gimmick loving
astrologers whose number has been increasing allover the world. I have
In the meantime. after the first edition of this book was published.
produced another book. "PredIcting through KMakdlTl5h.1 and Manclook
Oitsha".
The Sthiro Oosho
I tried to use the 5thlra dasha as is generally done and found that
while all horoscopes will have Rudra and Maheshwara, many w ill be
without Brahma. The steps are :
134

Predicting Ihroogh Jlimini Chlrl Oishi Conclusion with SuggeStlOllS
(a) Find out the stronger of the houses between the lagna and the
seventh.
(b) Then find out the strongest among the sixth. the eighth and the
twelfth lords, excluding Saturn, who never qualifies to be a
Brahma.
Upto this point all are unanimous.
The point of difference comes In when It is said that the planet In
the visible half and odd sign alone can qualify to be Brahma.
Try this method and there will be many horoscopes without
Brahma.
My problem was solved by a north Indian who
astroJoger~scholar
told me to find out the strongest among the sixth, eighth and the
twelfth lords from the stronger of the two houses between the lagna
and the seventh house, after excluding Saturn.
This scholar quoted from a commentary of Neelakantha on lalmlnl
sutras.
Not satisfied with his solution. I gave him some horoscopes of
dead persons and he calculated their span of life very accurately in many
of those cases by his method of doing Sthira dasha. I have followed that
method and found it giving me very good results even for predicting
good events of life. It is what I have taught to students and they too got
good results.
For me it has been a long grope through the darknesses of the
commentaries or translations of lalmlni Sutras. It shocked me to see that
a single writer ever stated that he had found lalrnlni system working and
proceeded to show It. In a train ,as I have described earl1er. I discovered
a due and worked on It and then the readings of Vemuri Ramamurthy
Shastri showed the posslbilitles of the vast areas of unexplored Ialmini
techniques. My success since then has been very good. Yet. how much
more Is to be Is what future generations will have to find
re~discovered
out. Mine Is an absolutely original and pioneertng work is all that I am
claiming. So I must conclude this book by stating very dearly that the
aim of this book is to try the predictive methods as shown by me ard
not treat me as a final authority on the subject.
